// -----// IR Dump After GPUDistributeForallPass (iree-codegen-gpu-distribute-forall) //----- //

  %0 = affine.linearize_index disjoint [%thread_id_z, %thread_id_y, %thread_id_x] by (1, 1, 32) : index
  %cst = arith.constant 0.000000e+00 : f32

  %c1024_i32 = arith.constant 1024 : i32

  %reinterpret_cast_0 = memref.reinterpret_cast %arg1 to offset: [%3], sizes: [1024], strides: [1] : memref<*xf32> to memref<1024xf32, strided<[1], offset: ?>>
  %reinterpret_cast_1 = memref.reinterpret_cast %arg0 to offset: [%3], sizes: [1024], strides: [1] : memref<*xf32> to memref<1024xf32, strided<[1], offset: ?>>
  %alloc = memref.alloc() : memref<1024xf32, #gpu.address_space<workgroup>>
  %c256 = arith.constant 256 : index
  %c0_2 = arith.constant 0 : index
  %c256_3 = arith.constant 256 : index
  %c128 = arith.constant 128 : index
  scf.for %arg4 = %c0_2 to %c256_3 step %c128 {
